A row carrying an unanswered question is found by its last cell rather than matched whole, so a row written with any number of brackets is answered at once rather than holding the run up. Every cell of a finding after its claim is read for citations, a disposition carrying evidence included. On the index: an entry leaning on a term another article owns, or teasing a count it never spends — one thing as much as several, and a count written before what it counts as much as after, though one thing taken out of several is no count at all — an entry being read before the article that defines its words. On the baseline page: a commit written there that is not the commit citations are read at, the page being a reader's account of which source supports the wiki. The cases of a `match` statement are arms like the halves of an `if`, and an arm that never ends at all — an endless loop — leaves even a `finally` unrun, so that `finally` is an alternative to it, while a `finally` that never ends is itself running and is no alternative to what entered it; a `finally` that leaves takes a branch out its own way, so a return under a `finally` that breaks is a break — where the branch's own way out crosses that `finally` at all: a break or a continue bound for a loop inside the `try` stays in there, the loop it reaches having to hold the branch, a loop whose `else` holds the branch having finished already, and what follows the loop runs before the `finally` does. The nearest `finally` is read first, and the way it leaves by is what the one around it is asked about, that being the way the branch is on by then. A `with` leaves by the way its body does, save for a raise its context manager could swallow, and bounds a raise inside it for the same reason. A way out written inside a function of a block's own waits on a call and leaves that rather than the block, while a class body runs where it stands and the raise that is the only way out of one leaves the block, by that way and on every path the body leaves on; a loop leaves where a break cannot reach it and its `else` leaves, a break under a `finally` that always leaves without breaking itself being overridden by it and reaching nothing, whichever ways that `finally` leaves by, or where it is an endless `while` whose body leaves, a `for` over something that may be empty reaching what follows it; one written inside a loop of its own leaves the loop where it is a `break` or a `continue`, and the block where it is a `raise` or a `return`, though one in that loop's `else` carries on the loop outside it. Each row's citations and marker words are read once rather than again for every pair of them, and a row carrying more references than can be weighed in pairs is reported as that rather than weighed, so a row written with thousands of them is answered rather than left running. A range running backwards is refused before anything else is asked of it, and a line number of any length is read, one too long to turn into a number at all being answered with a line no file reaches, so it is refused for running past the end rather than passed over or ending the run. A count written with no number in it is no count, and is read as nothing rather than taken for one; one too long to turn into a number is answered with a figure nothing counted could match, so it is reported as wrong rather than ending the run, and a span of register rows beyond counting names nothing rather than every number up to it. An `except*` bounds a raise as an `except` does, but its handlers are not arms of each other: one exception group can carry several kinds and run several of them, so only the `else` is their alternative, and each of them is tried while anything can arrive, the kind it names being worked out against what is left of the group even after an earlier one has caught. A handler's arm is its body alone, its own kind being worked out while the handlers after it are still candidates. A block written on a line the heading runs to shares that line with what the heading works out — a condition, a kind, a pattern, read to the last line it reaches — which runs whether the block does or not, so the line is no part of the alternative, and an arm left with no line of its own is an alternative to nothing. A `try`'s `else` runs where its body reaches the end, so a body that leaves skips the `else` and is the path itself. A handler's own ways stand beside the body's only where that body could raise at all, and a loop written so that its body never runs — a condition written out that never holds, `not` of one that does, an `and` or an `or` of such, or nothing written out to go over — is read for its `else` alone, nothing its body holds being read for a way out, though that `else` is read for a raise like any other block, as is whatever the loop goes over; one written so that its body runs at least once never finishes where that body always leaves; a condition or a case guard written out so that its answer is known leaves only the arm that runs to read, a case whose guard never holds being passed over and nothing after a case that always matches read at all; a comparison is weighed where each side is written out as a value of its own and the sign does not refuse the pair, chains included, stopping at the first pair that fails so that a side after it is never read; whether one thing is another is weighed only between what stands alone, two values written the same way being one object or two as the compiler settles it; what a loop goes over is read as the part an `and` or an `or` stands as, and handing each turn to anything but a plain name could raise; a value written out in full is read as that value, which leaves out a call, a name, and a set or a mapping holding what cannot be hashed, and going over what is written as something no turn can be taken from raises before any turn; an `if` or a `match` could raise only where working out what it is given could, or where the arm that runs could, a case a guard holds back and anything after a case that always matches being passed over there as well, a pattern being weighed before its guard is read, and weighing one could raise on a name it looks up; a loop written to go over what no turn can be taken from ends by the raise that takes, nothing in it running, while a loop that takes no turn hands nothing over to come apart, and an `else` the loop never reaches is read neither for a way out nor for a raise; an `and` or an `or` is read for raising in the parts that are worked out alone, and settles its answer only where every part before the settling one is written out; an assert is read where its test is written out so that its answer is known: a test that holds settles it, the message never being worked out, while one that fails raises the error it is for every time and leaves nothing after it to run, unless the message it carries could raise as it is worked out, which leaves the answer unknown; an arm that could raise where it stands reaches no further than such a raise would, a handler or a context manager between it and the end carrying that run on past it, and an ending that could raise as it runs bounds the arm the same way — a `finally` whose body could, or a `with`, whose own ending could whatever its body did — the handlers around such an ending being read as running after the arm whatever kind they name, since an ending that could raise without saying so names no kind to weigh them against, which loses the pair rather than inventing one — such a raise leaving the construct it came from rather than being caught by it, nothing inside that construct having a turn left either, and only where it stands inside the branch's own function; a `with` holding more than one manager keeps its place instead, an ending of one being swallowed by another in the same statement, which is also why such a statement can carry on whatever its body does; a return or a statement of nothing but a value written out could not raise at all, nor can such a value given to plain names, nor a raise of an error the language carries that any number of values will build and that is built as itself — a group and the errors about letters ask for an exact number, and the errors about the system around the program are chosen by the number they are given, so asking for one by name can hand back another — named plainly or called with values written out and nothing named by word, whose cause is nothing at all or such an error — the raise is then the way out it says it is and nothing more — nor a `try` nothing in which could raise: the kind each handler names is worked out as a raise arrives, in the order written until one catches what arrived, whether or not that handler then runs, every kind being weighed where the `try` takes errors in groups, and raises nothing of its own only where every kind named is an error the language itself carries, a handler of a `try` that takes errors in groups being refused where it names a group; a raise the handlers do not catch carries out, a handler that runs could raise of its own, and the `else` is asked only where the body reaches its end; names written out together take a value written out with a thing for each of them, whatever holds those things, and a starred name takes what is left over; a name given no value at all is still worked out where it is more than a name; and the handlers an arm could raise into are no alternative to it, however it leaves on its other paths, nor are the handlers around an ending that could raise as the arm leaves through it, that raise running them after the arm has gone; a handler naming a kind through anything but a plain name is read as able to run, nothing about it being weighable against the handlers before it; and a container something is unpacked into could hold anything, so it answers for nothing; a pattern naming a kind takes no more than that kind, whatever the kind is, the name being bound at the time the `match` runs, and weighing one could raise on that name — every alternative of an `|` is read for it, however wide the one after it; two arms inside one loop are alternatives for one turn, which a row citing both names as alternatives like any other pair; handlers are read in the order written: one whose every kind an earlier handler already catches cannot run, nor can one no raise of the body could arrive at — though a raise still looking for a handler arrives at any kind named through anything but a plain name, nothing about such a kind being weighable, and so does one naming no kind at all — a bare raise carries whatever is already being handled, which any handler could catch, so none of them is read as unable to run and no arm raising that way is an alternative to what follows it — and nothing after one naming every error can run either, though where the errors come in groups such a handler is still tried, the kind it names being worked out against what is left of one, so a kind that cannot be worked out raises there — and a handler of such a `try` naming a group of errors always does, the language refusing that pairing — a group being either of the two the language carries, unless the module gives that name something else, or a class the file writes on one of those — read by the name as written, since the class statement is the binding that makes it a group, and only where the module binds that name once: a class rebound afterwards holds whatever came last, and a scope of its own binding that name holds something else by then, so a handler inside it names no group, so its own body cannot run and nothing written after it is reached at all, while a body leaving on some paths only takes its own way out on those and the `else` on the rest and so names no single way; how a statement ends, and whether it could raise, is read once and kept against that statement, a nest of `try` statements otherwise being read again at every level and the gate stalling rather than answering; how a block ends is read as the set of ways out it has — a return, a raise, a continue, a break, or carrying on — gathered from every statement of it and from every block each of those holds, so a branch leaving two ways names neither and nothing is paired against it, while a one-sided `if` that returns before a later return names the return they agree on. A kind is weighed only where it is written as a plain name the file binds just once, by an import that brought the error itself — each alias an import writes counting as a binding of its own, so one name written twice in an import holds what came last, while an annotation with no value beside it binds nothing where the module carries it, though one written inside a function makes the name that function's own throughout as a deletion does, a type written out as a name of its own binds that name as a function or a class written to take a type binds it, while a class body's names are the class's own, so nothing written straight into one is counted — its methods and its own classes included, and a second import of the same thing displaces nothing: a parameter, an import, an assignment or a deletion of that name, or any name at all bound more than once — read against the scopes reaching the line it stands on, so a name one function or one class body binds leaves another scope's reading of it alone, an `if`, a `try` or a loop belonging to the scope around it while a function or a class written inside one starts a scope of its own — a deletion making it the function's own name throughout, reachable or not — leaves a handler naming it read as able to run and naming nothing another handler can be weighed against, since what it holds is not the error the language carries — and working that name out could raise as the handler is matched, so the handlers around it stand. A file holding a wildcard import has every such name kept, anything it brought in being unknowable. A raise is held with the kind it names, and a handler catches it only where it names no kind or names a kind that covers the one raised — a `KeyError` caught by `LookupError` as well as by its own name, the covering read off the errors the language itself carries, so `Exception` and `BaseException` catch whatever stands under each of them — a raise no handler of its own covers standing as a way out beside the handlers' own ways, and a kind named nowhere the language carries being left uncaught; the covering is read the same way wherever a handler is weighed — in what it catches, in what a `try`'s handlers catch between them, and in what an earlier handler has already caught — so a citation inside a handler nothing can arrive at, one every kind of which an earlier handler covers or one of a `try` whose body raises nothing at all, is refused as lines no input reaches, blank lines and comments padding such a citation answering for nothing — and its own line goes with its body only where no raise reaches it at all, a handler tried and missed having the kind it names worked out even though its body never runs, a body written on that same line being kept with the kind — a citation taking in one such handler whole, its own line and a line of its body, being refused with the body, while one spanning the whole `try` stands — and nothing written after a handler naming every error is reached at all, neither its body nor the kind it names; a context manager may swallow a raise or may not, so both stand — and a raise the body could take without saying so, out of a call or a name or a sum, is swallowed the same way, so carrying on stands beside the body's own ways for it. A raise reaches the handlers of the `try` holding it even where a `finally` takes the way out over, the handlers being weighed by the way the branch itself leaves rather than the way it is left on. A break need not be taken, so a loop holding one can still finish and run its `else`, and an endless `while` with no way out of it at all ends by nothing and reaches what a return would, and a return, a continue or a break in the body of a `try` leaves as it would anywhere, whatever its handlers catch. A logging call spread over several lines is logging on all of them. The register's own findings are read as evidence rows are, so a finding citing a docstring for behaviour, a line past the end of a file, a line with no file named before it, or two lines in opposite arms fails the same way an article would; a finding whose subject is the docstring may cite it, saying so, and a figure a finding means as a figure is written in words. Each is locked by [`test_check_wiki.py`](tests/test_check_wiki.py), which presents the defect and asserts the refusal, so a check that stops refusing fails there — and the gate is read for a name nothing in it reaches, so a check left behind by the one that replaced it fails there too rather than reading as live code — and every rule the page walk runs is presented a page breaking that one rule alone, so a rule left unwired fails too. Beside those, every pair the gate offers is weighed against what a pass through the code really runs: shapes are written out from the grammar the gate reasons about, run under a line trace once for each input, and a pair one pass reaches both sides of is reported — exhaustively over the small shapes a finding has ever come from — an arm in the body, a handler, an `else` or a `finally` of each construct, two handlers of one `try` over every pair of kinds, two cases of one `match` over every pair of patterns, an arm raising a kind its own handler covers, an arm leaving under an ending that could raise into the handlers around it, an arm whose own `try` raises with no kind named, an arm raising a name the shape itself has taken over, and an arm holding a `try` that takes its errors in groups — and over two thousand written at random besides, by [`reachable_pairs`](tests/support/reachable_pairs.py). The gate runs on a Praxis-only checkout, where a citation into Nexus is passed over as unread rather than failed and the ledger's Nexus half is left unchecked, so every Praxis-side check still blocks on a clone that has no Nexus beside it — and such a run says so in a note of its own, so a reader is told which half went unread rather than left to assume both were read

- Record U-03 in [`the register`](docs/wiki/register.md): a ladder cancelled before its first rung, reaching `_start_ladder` before its deadline, passes a zero slice total into the terminal emitter, `TradeOutcome` refuses a non-positive total, and the `ValueError` is caught and logged. No outcome is produced at the time. The command survives as an accepted command with no intent, so the next start classes it a boot orphan and reports it `REJECTED` — the caller is told, but only after a restart, and as a rejection rather than a cancellation

- Record what the wiki is not sure about in the register rather than asserting it: that the venue feed repairs a record written off after an unanswerable lookup appears in a docstring and in no body, and is marked unverified with the docstring named. The docstring saying a schedule change is lost on restart was traced to the contrary: the change is written down, read back on resume, and the amended slice plan and gap are what the run restarts on, so the docstring is recorded as stale against the debt entry that already records the fix

- Record 70 findings in [`the register`](docs/wiki/register.md) that tracing turned up, each carrying where it went: into the article that states it, or into a row of its own. Among them: an endless `reference_price` or `price` passes every check on the way in and breaks later — the first in the arrival-slippage division, the second at the venue call, where the exception is logged rather than turned into an outcome, so the command has no ending until the next start rejects it as an orphan. A `timeout` that is a floating-point NaN or infinity passes the same way. `_require_str` rejects only falsey values, so a list or an integer reaches a stored command's `trade_id` or `symbol` intact. A Scheduled VWAP amend naming weights is refused at the handler before its interval is read, losing both. A fill whose commission was charged in a third asset leaves the order record and the ledger disagreeing. And a replay's starting capital is truncated to a whole number for sizing while the report scores against the exact figure

### Change

- Pin `polars` below 2.0 in [`pyproject.toml`](pyproject.toml). `polars` 2.0 drops the `memory_map` argument from `read_ipc`, which [`arrow_price_store.py`](praxis/arrow_price_store.py) and [`load_replay_bars.py`](praxis/replay/load_replay_bars.py) both pass, so a resolver free to take 2.0 fails 27 tests and the type check on code written against 1.x. The pin states what the call sites already assume; moving them to 2.0 is its own change

- Group the wiki index for a reader rather than by the order it was written in. Two thirds of the articles sat under one heading taken from a register package name, so that group held whatever had been written at the time — starting a host, credentials, logging and the event spine were all filed as vocabulary — while real groups starved at one or two entries. The index now opens by saying what Praxis and Nexus each do and names five articles to read first, groups every entry under a heading a reader would look under, and closes by saying the wiki is about Praxis and where to look for a subject it has no article for. Articles filed where nobody would find them moved: a part-shown order is one send rather than several, an abandonment is a request a caller makes beside a change, a deadline covers a single order as well as one spread out, and the bars a strategy is priced against have nothing to do with a replay. The two groups that had grown to twelve and thirteen entries split by subject, and the pass that takes work off the queues now follows placement, which is where it happens. Thirty-seven entries that read as a sentence lifted from the article — "one ground", "nought", "step series", "the gap it works out for itself" — say what the reader will learn instead

- Refuse an index section named after a register package. The register sorts the work into packages, which is the order it was written in rather than anything a reader wants, and a package name standing over a group of entries is that order leaking into the index — the group under it then holds whatever was written at the time

the name a bracket's protective exit is given, one letter joined to the entry's name with a dash, nothing asked of what is handed in, callers in both the launcher and the manager sharing the one helper, and nothing in the Nexus checkout working the name out at all, so the side that reads it is handed it rather than deriving it, where the module’s own docstring claims the derivation is a contract both sides must compute identically; what the Nexus logging adds over the Praxis arrangement already written up, a binding that lasts a block and puts the keys it was given back as they stood, on the way out through a raise as much as through the end, where the level lookup and the emptying of the root are the same statements on both sides; and the three records that refuse nothing, declaring their fields and checking none of them, one of the three able to say whether its lifecycle has finished. Four further findings are recorded in the register as already written rather than written twice. 703 articles, 11,326 line references across 5,080 claims
- Add seven articles on the Nexus side's fourteenth package, the five records left on that side with refusals in them, none of which any article cited before: what the event logged so a count of losses can be rebuilt is checked for, its strategy's reference and the kind it names each having to be text with something left once trimmed, its moment a moment carrying the universal-time marker itself, and the result it carries a finite decimal figure, where a whole number is refused however ordinary and no check of the record's own weighs that figure against nought; the one field of that event allowed to be empty text where a blank one is refused, the check asking whether the text has content before it trims it and the field having no none at all, so empty text is the only way the record can say the field was not filled in; what the signal a predictor hands back is checked for, its reference, a dictionary of values whose every key must be text and whose whole, floating and decimal values must each be finite, with a value of any other kind weighed in no way at all, and its moment held to the same marker; the sealing three records on that side do by one statement each, copying the mapping they were handed and sealing the copy over the field it arrived in, which stops a caller changing which entries the record holds and leaves every entry pointing at the object the caller handed in, so reaching into one of those and altering it alters what the record holds — closing a candidate the eleventh package's re-seed left open — with a signal crossing a process boundary handed over unsealed and rebuilt on the far side by running every one of its checks again; what the settings a strategy is configured with ask and answer, one check that what was handed is a dictionary and nothing else weighed, and a missing setting answered with whatever default the asking named; what the word that a bracket's protection was lost and dealt with is checked for, four fields walked by name and each held to the same rule, a moment and its marker weighed by one test rather than two, and an attempt number that must be a whole number at or above one with a true-or-false caught by a test set ahead of the one for whole numbers; and the three limits that trip the breakers, each able to be left out and passed over before its own figure is looked at, where a limit that is given must be a finite decimal figure above nought, so nought is refused and leaving a limit out is the only way to leave it unset. 703 articles, 11,326 line references across 5,080 claims
- Add seven articles on the Nexus side's thirteenth package, the two records Nexus keeps around an order, whose own checks no article reached before: what the record of money held is checked for, its own reference and the reference of the strategy that asked for it each having to be text with something left once trimmed and each of its two sums a finite figure at or above nought, where both checks refuse a figure below nought rather than one standing at it; the moment it was made at and the moment it runs out at, each having to carry the universal-time marker itself and the second having to stand strictly after the first, these being the only pair of its fields it weighs against each other, so a record cannot run out at the instant it was made; what it is asked once it exists, being the sum of its two figures, worked out on each reading rather than read from a field and named by none of its own checks, and whether it has run out, which refuses a moment handed in from outside where every other refusal of the record weighs a field the record holds, and answers yes at the moment it runs out as well as past it; what the record of the attempt must carry, a granted one something in its lock field and nothing in its reason field and a refused one the reverse, as four checks standing on their own, none of which asks the type of any of its three fields where the record beside it asks the type of all six of its own; what the note Nexus needs to read a report is checked for, two references that must be usable text and a third held to the same rule wherever it is given, a side that must be one of the named ones, two yes-or-no fields that must be yes-or-no, and a worth and an expected fee that must each be a finite figure with the worth above nought and the fee only at or above it; the size that note may leave out, absent only where the order grows a position, an absence clearing that one check, and otherwise a finite figure above nought, those two tests reading nowhere the flag that allowed the absence; and the flag saying the order grows a position, which must be a yes-or-no, never weighed against the side so that a buy may be marked either way and a sell too, read turned round to answer whether the order reduces a position, and unable to stand true alongside the other yes-or-no the note carries, a note setting both being refused by that check, which either of them standing false clears. 703 articles, 11,326 line references across 5,080 claims
- Add four articles on the Nexus side's twelfth package, the report the trading side sends back, which no article reached before: what every one of those reports checks on itself, its own reference and the reference of the instruction it answers each having to be text with something left once trimmed, its kind one of the named ones, its moment a moment carrying the universal-time marker itself, and a size still working, where one is given, a finite figure at or above nought, that last check being reached after the report has already been sorted by whether anything filled and still asked of every kind, so the checks every report answers sit either side of that sorting rather than all of them before it; what a report of a fill must carry, a size, a price, an amount and the fees paid, each refused where absent and refused again where not a finite figure, three of them having to stand above nought where the fees need only be at or above it, and the same four forbidden to a report carrying no fill, each refused on its own; the reason such a report may carry, where a refusal must give one with something left in it once trimmed while a cancellation may give one or leave it out, each of the two refused on every kind but its own; and the two measures it may carry, taken by name in one loop where an absent one is passed over before any figure is looked at while a measure of nought goes on to the checks, refused on a report carrying no fill, and required to be a finite figure where the report carries one, so on a report carrying no fill an absent measure passes where a measure of nought is turned away. 703 articles, 11,326 line references across 5,080 claims
- Add four articles on the Nexus side's eleventh package, the instruction Nexus hands the trading side, which no article reached before: what that instruction checks on itself as it is made, its reference, account, venue and symbol each having to be text with something left once trimmed, its kind one of the three named ones, the amount it names a finite figure at or above nought and its moment a moment, with thirteen further things that may be left out refused wherever present and wrong, every one of those tests standing behind a test for absence, and a deadline given as a true-or-false caught by a test set ahead of the one for whole numbers because such a thing would otherwise pass as one; the marker its moment must carry, which is the universal-time marker itself rather than any zone standing at that offset, so two moments naming one instant are treated differently and the other handoffs the same connector makes accept the zone this one refuses; the settings it rewrites, where anything that is not a mapping is refused and a mapping is copied, sealed and written over its own field, so a caller altering what it handed over changes nothing the instruction carries, this being the one check of the module that changes rather than refuses; and what each kind must carry, a new order naming a side, a self-trade setting and either a size or a spend but never both or neither, where a change and a cancel may carry none of those eleven things at all, each kind weighed against its own copy of that list rather than one they share. 703 articles, 11,326 line references across 5,080 claims

- Add six articles on the Nexus side's tenth package, the strategy cluster, which no article reached before: the wrapper every strategy sits behind, built for it alone as the start builds the strategy and holding a lock no other strategy's shares, which every way in takes and holds across the call, so a strategy reached through its wrapper is asked one thing at a time while the lock one strategy is held under is not the lock another is held under; which roads reach a strategy, eight of them, each reached outside the tests from a single line — three where a start begins it, hands back the state it saved and replays an event to it, two where a stop asks it to finish and asks for its state to keep, and one each for a prediction, an outcome and a timer — every one of those lines on the Nexus side, with Praxis's own modules naming none of them; what a strategy must provide, seven things it has to supply before it can be built at all and an eighth already supplied whose body discards the record it is handed; what the thing routing to a strategy refuses, three things when it is asked for one by name and five more when the whole set is built, with the name trimmed both as the wrappers are taken in and as one is looked up; what a turn of a timer does, set going by a countdown that fires once, marked so it cannot hold the process up and kept under a name joining the strategy and the timer, testing whether the loop still runs before it fetches what the strategy is handed and again after, asking for the next turn even where the work raised but building no countdown where the loop has stopped by then; and what a strategy is handed, a sealed thing checked five ways before a strategy sees it, its own fields unable to be rebound once built, with the settings beside it built empty on every road that hands any. 703 articles, 11,326 line references across 5,080 claims

- Add three articles on the Praxis side's sixth package, which is what verifying the fifth turned up rather than a module with no page: what the clearing of the notes saying an instruction's figures are unsaved cannot reach, that clearing working from a list copied before the save and testing only the entries the list holds, so a note for one instruction can fall outside the list another instruction's save copies and be left standing by it, where a note always falls inside the list its own save copies because it goes on before that reply is queued, with the clearing being the one deletion the list of notes ever takes, and such a note standing for figures that save wrote down or for figures it did not with no way to tell the two apart; what the lock over the account's money is doing when a sale's result is written down, which is nothing, by either of the two routes that reach that line: where the sale frees money set aside, the lock is taken inside the freeing and handed back as it answers, before the line goes down; and where it frees none, nothing reaches for the lock at all and the line is still written; and what a save of the whole picture waits on, the lock it takes over the account's money being the money's own, made once and handed out by a reading, which ten of the money's roads take, the one that sets money aside raising five refusals before taking it and then holding it from the clearing out of what has expired through to whichever ending it reaches, on a road that asks for no save of any kind itself. A fourth was drafted and withdrawn: the save a left-over note can ask for turned out to rest entirely on facts already written, the test's third arm and the acknowledgement that sets neither of the other two, so the register carries the finding and no article does. 703 articles, 11,326 line references across 5,080 claims

- Add seven articles on the Praxis side's fifth package, the half of the seam between the two systems that the Nexus run left open: how a note that an instruction's figures are unsaved is cleared, which happens in batches, so one instruction's save clears the notes standing against others while a save an operator asks for clears none; what can stop the line that closes a reply going down, the ten-second wait that leaves the write still going and the road that never attempts the line at all; what ends a start squaring its holdings, which is a holding the trading side does not mention; which starts weigh nothing against nothing and so ask for no save; the road an arriving reply takes and what it never reads, with the one account road that drops a reply and the one that applies its figures later; when the part that saves state runs with neither of the two locks over what it reads, being the window before the start has handed them over; and which of its three kinds of save take which of those locks. 703 articles, 11,326 line references across 5,080 claims

- Add five articles on the Nexus side's ninth package, which hosts save their state and the roads into the log nothing takes: which hosts the save-on-a-timer job runs on, switched on only for a live account as that account's own thread starts its background work and switched off again on the way down, where a run over bars that already happened builds the same machinery, never switches it on, and never reaches the save a stopping host makes either, so a full picture gets written on such a run only where its own start asks for one, though the run still writes single changes as it works replies through; what a failed save costs that start, which the first article had no room to hold once it outgrew its cap, the failure caught where the save is asked for and turned into a start failure that nothing on the way out catches, left to the account's own thread on a live host and to the run's closing step on a run over past bars, which shuts the account down before the failure leaves; which clock a line in the log is stamped off, always the real time of the machine, because the part that writes those lines is built without a clock and handed none, so a run driven by a made-up clock stamps real time onto its records while the money, the checks, the modes and the predictions all read the made-up one; the road that empties the log, wiping the file complete with its opening mark, which nothing in either system calls, and which the next write would repair rather than refuse; and what writes the name a success records, the label of a reply from the venue kept so a repeat of it can be turned away, which nothing saves on its own account: the save a host asks for after a reply turns on three marks that keeping the label sets none of, so the label goes to disk only on the back of the next save something else asked for, and a host that stops with no save of any kind behind it leaves a log and a picture that never held the label, while the turning away of a repeat reads that label where it stands in memory, so the wait costs nothing until a restart. Add four facts to articles already written: the position a start takes in from Praxis, which rides the same test as the two changes that article already names and is written nowhere else either; the moving line the clearing of old entries draws, read off the clock as the clearing runs, so an entry one clearing keeps the next one throws away with nothing about the log having changed in between; the six places turning a line back into a record can fail, where one catch inside the step covers a missing field alone; and the removal in the account thread that reaches nothing where the build it wraps is what failed, the runtime only reaching the list it removes from once that build has returned

- Add eight articles on the Nexus side's eighth package, the log and the figures that reach it: how a record reaches the log, each line given a short head saying how long it is and a number worked out from its contents, forced out to the disk before the write is called done, a file whose end was left torn trimmed back to the last sound line before anything new goes on, and a file whose opening mark reads wrong refused outright; what a damaged record costs, the read stopping at the first line that fails any of its checks, a short head, a length past the cap, a length past the file's end, a payload that runs out or a checksum that does not match and keeping only what came before it, so one bad line hides every good line after it, alongside a second way of reading the file that raises where the first keeps quiet and that no code under the package calls, which left with the unpack fault as an eighth article when the subject outgrew its cap; which of a host's changes reach the log, an operator halting or resuming the whole account written down every time and an outcome from the venue only where working it through succeeded and one of three marks is set, while the running valuations of what is held are written on nobody's account and ride along with whatever else is saved; how the checkpoint rewrites the log, emptying the file and writing the survivors back into that same file with no side file and no move, so a failure part-way through leaves the file holding less than the history it stood for, a file too short to carry the opening mark passing the one guard unread; the timer that asks for those checkpoints while a host runs, measuring each wait from the end of the last save and passing over a failed save, the turn after it making a whole new one rather than finishing the last; the cap on how fast entries may be let by, counted in one allowance for the whole account so a busy strategy can spend it all and leave the others turned away; and the window that turns a repeated order away, keeping labels against the strategy that sent them for one second, its refusals for an order naming no side and for one naming no label never reached from a live request, Praxis filling both in before it builds one

- Add six articles on the Nexus side's seventh package, the last of stopping and what sits either side of the checks: what a stopping host saves of its strategies, asking each to describe itself in bytes and writing each to a side file and moving it over the old one, a strategy that cannot describe itself leaving the file it had before standing; the last record it writes, a final picture with the log behind it cut back to recent activity, then the account handed back, each step wrapped so a failure is written down and passed over; what the first stage weighs before it reads trading state, which is a name with space around it, a request worth nothing and a strategy whose whole allowance is nothing, the name and the allowance already settled when the settings are read while a request to close or to alter an order is named as exempt from the amount test and the allowance test rather than having to clear them; what Praxis works out for a request to sell before the checks see it, from the holding being there to what is left of it after anything already in progress, which is where a full close is settled; the watch over handovers that ran out of time, which reports the flags that have stood too long while a command whose final reply the books cannot take in drops off the list with its flag still raised, one whose reply blows up on the way in leaving both standing; what the prediction loop's stop leaves running, since the loop gathers its work before it carries it out, so work gathered before the switch-off is still carried out and can still reach a strategy and put its answer up for the checks; and, split out of the first, what a start does with a strategy's saved state, handing empty bytes over for a file that is missing and for one it cannot read, and never looking at all where the setting is blank — which is the setting the stopping side falls back on and the start does not; and, split out of the second, what a checkpoint holds and what the thirty-day cut leaves, the cut going by when each log line was written, the recent-loss figures rebuilt from what survives going by when the thing described happened, and the running total adding up what survived once repeats of one outcome are dropped; and, split out of the third, what settles a request before the first stage, the name fixed when the settings are read while the allowance is written as the order is put together, from two figures the settings already hold above zero, save on a request to alter an order, which is given none at all; and, split out of that, what an order to buy has its worth worked out from, either an amount of money or a quantity trimmed to what the venue trades in before it is priced, which can make the order smaller than was asked for; and, split out of the first, what a failed save costs the stopping, a write that fails on the disk losing one strategy's update while leaving what it saved before, a directory that cannot be made losing every update, and a strategy handing back something the writer cannot take costing the shutdown its final saved picture while the account is still handed back

- Add six articles on the Nexus side's sixth package, stopping a Nexus host: what a stopping host halts first, turning away anything a strategy asks for through the usual checks and then switching off the background jobs one after another in a set order; and, split out of that, what the halted mode turns away, which is a strategy's request to open, close or alter, refused by the usual checks, while an abandonment and the system's own closing orders still go out on routes that skip them; and, split out of it again, what a loop's stop leaves running, which cancels the next run of a repeating job and not the run already going, so a write can still land after the switch-off returns; what a strategy is handed when it is asked to stop, which is the holdings it owns, the money it still has room for and the state trading is in, with its own settings arriving empty; the abort a host sends as it stops, naming an order already handed over by a label the strategy supplies and going out with no checks asked at all; what a stopping host waits for, which is each order it wrote down rather than each order it sent, read until the list clears, a set time passing with some unfinished bringing a drop and half as long again before the host goes down regardless; where an outcome outside the wait is sent, which is a stand-in the launcher wires for any reply whose order is off that list, reached once the usual reader has been told to stop; and the name an order comes back with, settled once by reading the receiving side for a label of its own, so a label that differs is a failure for one receiver and simply the label for another; and, split out of the first of those, stopping the loop that reads outcomes, which is asked to finish and given a while, one still going when the while is up being left to complete the turn it is in and so able to take one more message away from whoever reads next; and, split out of the last, which name a command is kept under, filed under the label the sender chose where the other side was held to it and under the label that came back where it was not; and, split out of that, claiming the money after the send, the road taken where the receiving side is not held to the label it was sent, so a refusal arrives with the order already handed over and can leave the reply to it with no strategy to attach it to — a road the launcher's own wiring never takes, since the receiver it hands over does declare that label

- Add six articles on the Nexus side's fifth package, the passes that tidy up: when a reservation times out, swept by a walk at the head of a fresh request while an order finding its own hold already expired hands the money back itself; releasing an order the system can name and no longer describe, which answers success for an order that was never there; whether a fill closes a position, a leftover below the increment the venue prices sizes in counting as closed; the sale a host sends as it stops, asked for by a strategy and approved by the host itself so the checks that normally stand in front of an order never see it; and where an imported position's three labels come from, a holding missing any one of them being left out of the records altogether; and what weighs a shutdown sale, which is the price guard on the single-shot road and the venue's own minimum size as the order is placed, each skipped where its own settings are absent and the first skipped entirely by a sale fed out in pieces

- Add seven articles on the Nexus side's fourth package, the figures that follow a position: what moves a strategy's committed figure, ten places moving it and one meaning to raise it; what a pending sale is, which caps what a further sale asking through the checks may request; what a position written back at a start carries, taking one price as both what it was entered at and what its money is booked at; what the host counts when a fill empties a position, a holding being gone before the message announcing its close arrives; and what a pending sale does to the two roads into the dust, barring the one for a close the venue refused and leaving the one for a finishing fill wide open; and what a settling fee does to the committed figure, which it can raise with no money set aside behind the rise; and what a pending sale refuses, which the sales a host fires off as it shuts down never meet

- Add twelve articles on the Nexus side's third package: where a position's last scraps go, and the running total per coin that no decision reads; what money arriving in the account changes, which is the log and the mark that the report was seen, two pools being kept, the one the checks weigh against fixed when the account is born and the one a strategy is told it has taken from the settings; where a realized result is written down, and why the record is written before the figures are counted; what a boot finds stranded, and what writing it off gives up; and what a boot rebuilds from the positions, without which money set aside is refused for as long as the per-strategy figures do not add up; what a dusted scrap leaves behind in the money figures; and which fills come back with a result at all, the gain being measured from the price a position was entered at rather than the one its money is booked at; and what two records of committed money disagreeing refuses, which is a new holding and not a sale; and how much a taking-out takes, a strategy left with a sliver being dropped from the books rather than kept; and what a start does with a position written off, which is to ask the host what it holds and write back what the record is missing, passing over anything it cannot read; and what a start weighs each position at, the price it settles on being written back onto the position; and what the fee figure taken off a gain is worked out from, which is a flat thousandth of the fill, while the ledger on the same side books the charge the venue reported

- Ask `_check_page` itself which checks an atom and a spine are held to, each check replaced in turn by one that answers, where the test read the shape of the two branches out of the source. A guard that can never hold reads there as one that always does: the atoms branch was an early return, so its checks sat under no test at all, and pointing either guard at the folder rather than its name, inverting one, or exchanging the two all left the suite green. Each check is counted rather than merely heard, a check called twice in one branch being the defect the test exists for

- Check that the causes and the obligation words the register names are the ones the purpose rule matches, the pattern spelt out into the phrases it accepts and read against the register's own sentence, and anything in a pattern that cannot be spelt out stopping the reading rather than being guessed at. The register named four where the pattern took eight, the causes were read against nothing at all, and a word appended after the sentence's last `or` went unseen

- Read a module's logger off the name it assigns `getLogger` to, where the rule refusing a citation that holds nothing but a logged message looked for `_log` alone. That is the Praxis name and most of Nexus keeps it, but the module holding the money figures writes `_logger`, and a range holding one of its warnings and nothing else read as code. Structlog's `get_logger` is read alongside `logging.getLogger`, two Nexus modules taking the second. A line carrying `else:`, `try:` or `finally:` and nothing more no longer saves such a range either

- Count a Nexus module as owned by the article identifiers its ledger cell carries, where the absence of `cited, not traced` was read instead. Two cells came to carry both, which counted them as owned by nobody and put the split wrong in three places at once, with no count able to fail

- Check that a Nexus ledger cell already carrying rows carries every row that cites the file. The Praxis cells were held to their citations exactly and the Nexus cells to only half of that, so 22 rows across 13 cells had gone unlisted; a cell reading `cited, not traced` carries none and is held to neither side

- Check each row identifier a Nexus ledger cell carries, which has to belong to an article citing that file. Membership was checked both ways and identity not at all, so two cells naming an article that cited neither of their files sat there unseen. The test takes the cell carrying the most rows, a cell carrying one being unable to tell every offender from the first

- Put the newest bullet at the top of this version's Add section, where the one standing there was the oldest

- Gather this version's three `### Add` headings into one, in the order the house keeps — Add, then Fix, then Change. Successive commits had each opened a new heading rather than adding to the one above, which no other version in the file does

- Harden the gate against the two voice defects it was given, which took seven readings of the diff to get right. An ordinal standing in for a thing is refused in its definite form alone — a third held in reserve is a fraction, a second is usually a length of time, and no pattern separates those from the defect — and one following a first in the same section is left alone, the reader having the list in front of them. A purpose clause carrying an obligation is refused where the two sit in one clause, the window stopping at a full stop, a comma, a bracket, a bracket of either kind, a dash of any of four, or a word that opens another clause, and reads an obligation only as a word of its own rather than inside a hyphenated one — *that* excepted, the real defect carrying a relative clause of its own, which leaves one known false positive — an obligation inside a noun phrase — that the writer rewords around, which lets a compound subject past. Both read whole paragraphs — a list item being its own, quoted or not, so a cause in one and a requirement in the next are not paired — so a sentence that wraps is read as one, both read a phrase however widely its words are spaced, and both read a word through the emphasis around it. Both judgements go the same way: a check that blocks correct prose costs more than one that misses

- Fix the defects those readings found in the rest of the gate. A review stamped later than the last edit is refused as covering a version that never existed, one with no stamp or no modified line is refused without crashing, and a stamp written twice or with no version is refused rather than read as the first of them. The state column is read whatever case it is written in, so a state the wiki does not use is refused rather than skipped. A review stamp may sit anywhere in the metadata, including past the evidence list, but not inside it, where dropping it would hide a claim parted from its source. And the Nexus breakdown is checked as well as its totals, in the ledger's own words as well as the index's — three pages stated how the named modules split and all three had it wrong

- Read a row span written with the Nexus series, and refuse one whose ends are not the same series and package — the end was matched and thrown away, so a span from one series to another expanded as though both were the first. Row identifiers were widened to carry it and the span between two of them was left reading the Praxis series alone. A Nexus range had named nothing, every row in it reading as uncited

- Refuse a moment written into a page's prose, on every page rather than the articles alone. Stamping an article means rewriting the line carrying its time, and one such rewrite landed inside a paragraph on the register that quotes the footer convention, leaving the sentence half-replaced and a code span open for four days. It reads whole paragraphs too, so a moment split across a wrap is still found, and passes over what a fence holds, whichever mark opens it and however long, a shorter run inside a longer one not closing it, nor a run of the other mark, nor one naming a language, where a longer run of the same mark alone does; a quote mark is taken off before any of this, so a fence or a paragraph break inside a quotation is read as one, and a quotation ending closes a fence it opened, however it ends. A thematic break parts two paragraphs the way a blank line does, prose and a quotation after it are two, and a backtick run opening a list item is not read as a fence — that was tried and was wrong, taking three list items for one fenced block and hiding the middle one. The footer on its own line, a metadata key, a table cell and a pinning line are left alone, each matched whole; a footer quoted inside a sentence is not, nor is a sentence that merely opens with those words

- Repair that paragraph, and the status line above it, which still named two states the gate refuses

- Hold every per-page check to running on both kinds of page by reading the branches off the source. One had been added at the wrong indentation, landing in the spines branch twice and the atoms branch not at all, which is valid Python and ran green for a day. A branch is told by the condition it sits under, and by that condition asking which folder the page is in and saying yes, so neither replacing it with a constant nor reversing its operator leaves the reading unchanged

- Make every test that could not fail able to. Among them: both heading cases wrote a heading the check would not have fired on anyway, so neither protected the exemption; no case wrapped a sentence across two lines, which is what reading paragraphs is for; the wiring test asserted only that more than eight checks were found where eleven exist; no metadata case carried a review stamp; no state case was written in capitals; no count was put on the index, so the arm reading it went unexercised. Each voice pattern had one example, leaving most of its words, every ordinal past the fourth and both its cases unpinned; they are written out member by member now, with a test that the written list is the one the pattern holds — the first attempt read the list off the pattern itself, so removing a word removed its own test; no case moved the unreviewed count while leaving the reviewed one right, so half that comparison could have been dropped; and the window cases sat well inside and well past the limit rather than either side of it, so any of thirty-five lengths would have satisfied both; the list-item case used the one marker the window already stopped at, so it proved nothing about splitting items; the three voice checks were reached only by calling them directly, so dropping any from the page walk changed nothing; and the purpose check's less common words had no case at all, so deleting lest, ought, needs to or cannot be allowed would have passed

- Correct two ledger rows naming an article that cites neither file: the ledger check reads which files a row names and not which rows name a file, so a stale identifier sits there unseen

- Close the review count on a word boundary, which it had been missing: "151 notes" and "151 nothing" both read as the count of rows not reviewed

- Correct a docstring on the ordinal check that still gave the indefinite form as the sentence it exists to catch, which the check stopped reading two rounds earlier

- Correct two register rows that counted: one said two checks read the order where four do, and one named four refusals where the stage has six ways to refuse

- Correct two index entries that described their articles as those articles read before the verify rounds: the checks do not mostly read the account, and the roads around them are not two

- Mark six more rows reviewed, both re-derivations having come back settled on the same text: how Nexus sets money aside, what becomes of it, what it does when a command does not get across, what each check weighs, and what the platform ceilings and the price check weigh. Each stamp names the version it covers, so editing any of them takes the mark off

- Separate whose catches are whose in the undoing article. Nexus's are for ordinary failures and an interrupt goes straight past them, where Praxis catches one once the claim has run, undoes what it can, and lets it go on

- Correct two last ones. The health stage's thresholds are each optional too, so a figure with none set is read and never acted on. And Praxis reads the clock before it takes the book from the cache rather than before fetching it — the poller stamps its own time after the fetch returns

- Correct two more. The lifetime peak the risk stage reads is of closed money alone, not of the measure the moment's figure uses. And a book stamped later than the snapshot need not mean either figure is wrong: Praxis reads the clock before it fetches, so a book landing between the two is genuinely newer

- Correct two more. A stage raising does not always leave money set aside: the stage that sets it is fourth of six, so only the two after it can raise with any held. And the four drawdown measures are two pairs — the moment's figure and a lifetime peak, each as an amount and a fraction — rather than four unlike things

- Correct two more. An undoing that fails under the send brings no release; one that fails escaping the taking does bring it, and the release then finds the reservation gone. And say that the four drawdown measures are no two of them alike, the worst on closed money refusing while the moment's figure reads nothing

- Narrow the unreachable-sign claim. A holding the account did not buy does count against the projected sum, positions being kept on either side; it is the negation written for the incoming order that no road reaches

- Say thirty days rather than a month for the third loss window, the windows being twenty-four hours, seven days and thirty

- Say that the order's own ceiling is conditional too. Every one of the platform stage's five is unset by default, so a stage with no ceilings configured passes everything

- Correct four more. A claim naming an order already tracked raises before the sweep and sweeps nothing, so not every claim catches up the figures. A registration raising before it claims leaves the money to be given back by the release; one raising after it leaves the release nothing to find. The later-stamp refusal comes before any subtraction, so there is no age below nothing to speak of. And the money is set aside before the decision is sent rather than before Praxis sees it — Praxis is holding the action throughout

- Correct two more. A registration raising before the claim drops itself first, so nothing is the wrong word for what it undoes, and a claim refused for being too late has already given its own amount back — either way the release that follows may find the reservation gone. And the signs in the projected holding are never exercised down this road: the stage is skipped for every action but an entry, and an entry has to buy

- Correct four more. A registration raising before the claim reaches the release with nothing undone, where one raising after it undoes the order first and leaves the release to find the reservation gone. The undoing drops any bracket exit recorded against the command as well. The projected holding negates the order's own amount where it sells. And a limit set on a figure nothing supplies refuses a decision that reaches that check, which is not every decision — an earlier refusal stands the rest down

- Correct two more. Intake's demand that a change carry an amount above nought is answered before it is asked: what Praxis puts there is a standing one, the same for every change. And the projected holding does not always include the order — one sized in money alone is turned into an amount at a middle price, and where none is to be had it adds nothing

- Correct three more. Two bookkeeping refusals come before the four limits: money deployed with no record of which strategy holds it, and a record that does not add up to the deployed total. A registration that raises as it is being taken does reach the plain release, the raise being caught where the taking is, so only a send failing with a registration already in hand skips it. And say what the projected holding is: what the order's own pair holds, bought counted positive and anything else negative, plus the order itself, floored at nothing

- Say what the book's age actually measures. The moment on the book is when Praxis fetched it, taken off its own clock as the fetch returned, the venue's answer carrying no time at all — so the age is the wait since the fetch rather than since the venue drew the book up

- Correct three more. Claiming a reservation takes its own amount out of the set-aside bucket and leaves every other reservation's there. An undoing in Praxis runs a callback against the position rather than restoring it, and drops the command's registration where this attempt wrote it. And the deviation's four refusals come before it is weighed against the cap, not before it is read at all — the missing-figure test reads it

- Correct four more. A reservation the record no longer holds is refused as not found whether it was given back, swept, or claimed already, so the answer covers three causes rather than two. An undoing in Praxis puts the position back and drops this attempt's records whatever happened, and only the order's money waits on the reservation having been claimed. A change's new amount is never weighed against the standing one at all, the only arithmetic for it sitting in the stage a change skips. And the price stage refuses alike for no snapshot, no moment on it and no stamp on the book, the later-stamp refusal being separate

- Correct two more. Marking a registration unknown is not nothing: in Praxis it writes a record against the command, and leaves the money alone. And the deviation has a fourth thing it refuses on unweighed — a decision carrying no reference price at all

- Withdraw the tally half of that check after one pass over every article. It cannot separate a count of a set the code enumerates, which is a fact, from the writer's grouping, which goes stale, and refusing both would have meant rewriting a dozen correct sentences. What survives is the ordinal with no first before it in the same section, which left three real instances to fix

- Fix the ordinal check, which had been added at the wrong indentation: it landed in the spines branch twice and in the atoms branch not at all, which is valid Python and ran green for a day. Hold both branches to their checks by reading them off the source, proved by putting the misindentation back and watching the test name both halves

- Say that past the claim there is nothing in the set-aside bucket to give back, and that what a failed send undoes is the tracked order instead

- Split the price check out as well, the third subject to outgrow the stage survey. It weighs the book's age, its spread and a price's distance from a reference, each only where a limit for it is set, and refuses a decision for a figure being absent as readily as for one out of bounds: a missing stamp, a book stamped later than the snapshot, a missing spread, a missing reference source, and two sources naming different things

- Correct three more. A failed claim drops the registration only where it came back a failure; where it raises instead, for an order already tracked, the registration is left standing. Marking a registration unknown undoes nothing in Praxis, which is a fact about Praxis rather than about every registration. And the lock around the projected holding is held only long enough to copy the list of positions: the positions in that copy are the live ones, their sizes written to as reports come in, so the figure can be read mid-change

- Record that a sweep takes a reservation at the expiry moment itself, where a claim arriving at that same moment is still in time

- Split the platform ceilings into their own article, a reviewer having found what makes them worth one. The stage weighs five things: what the order comes to, read off the decision, and four figures handed in from a snapshot. The snapshot Praxis builds carries the projected holding and nothing else, and a limit set where the figure is absent refuses the decision — so the limits offered on the order rate, the day's losses and the pool committed cannot be used to tighten the account, only to stop it

- Correct three more. A failed claim drops the registration only where that attempt put it there, and the raise is caught and reported as a failure to submit rather than travelling out. The registration handed an undoing holds no decision, only whether the reservation was claimed and which command it was. And the price stage's missing-figure refusals each need their own limit set, an unset one letting the figure go unread

- Correct two more. A registration owns an undoing only where the send failed other than by timing out; a timeout marks it unknown instead. And each of the price stage's three refusals fires on a missing figure as well as a bad one, the age refusing a book stamped later than now

- Name who claims a reservation, the earlier withdrawal having been right about Nexus and incomplete about the system. Praxis claims it as it registers the command — before the send goes out, not at it — and where that claim fails the registration is dropped and the submission raises. Nexus offers a road of its own to the claim and no code there walks it

- Correct three more over-assertions. A claim naming an order already tracked raises before the reservation is looked up at all, so the shared not-found answer covers only one given back or swept. The registration that owns an undoing comes from outside and is handed the decision with its reservation on it, so what the undoing reaches is decided out there rather than known to be untouched. And the health stage is not a pure pass-through: which way each figure is weighed, and that a halt outranks a breach and a breach a warning, are its own, a warning refusing nothing

- Correct two more. The eighty-per-cent weighing counts what this order and its fees come to alongside everything already deployed, rather than the deployed total alone. And where a registration owns the undoing, the money set aside is still set aside: nothing has claimed it on that road, so what the registration undoes is whatever else it took on

- Correct four more, two of them rules of this project broken by its own author. The health stage's figures were described as a round trip to the venue and a count of its failures, which is what their names say and not what the code shows: five figures are handed in from outside and weighed against thresholds handed in with them. And the striking claim that one limit governs two measures in two places was wrong — the mode controller keeps its own set, tied to the walk's by nothing, so one can be loose where the other is tight. Also: a reservation can be given back by name, a third ending beside claiming and sweeping, and a claim made too late has its own amount swept with the rest

- Correct four more. Four of the six checks read the order and the other two read the account's losses and how the venue is answering, so the grouping that called most of it account state was wrong twice over. The health stage stops a decision for the state of the connection — a round-trip time, a run of failures, rate-limit headroom, clock drift — and not for anything about the account's money. The value that leaves open positions out still counts what the account started with. And the worst the account has ever been down on its whole value is not unread: no stage of the walk sees it, and the part of Nexus deciding whether the account may trade weighs it against the same two limits the walk applies to the worst on closed money, so one configured limit governs two measures in two places

- Correct four claims about a command that does not get across. A release asked for without a controller to ask returns having released nothing, so both roads that ask are conditional. An undoing that raises leaves the figures wherever the registration had got to, rather than untouched. Which road a timeout takes turns on whether a registration was taken, nothing about the money being read. And only a timeout with a registration is reported unknown carrying the command's name; one without is reported failed, carrying none

- Correct three claims about money set aside, then split what becomes of it into its own article. The eighty-per-cent cap leaves the fee reserve out of what it counts and that reserve comes off free capital all the same, so the last fifth is not held clear of everything. Expired reservations are swept only where one is asked for or claimed — releasing one sweeps none, acknowledging an order sweeps none — so one nobody claims counts against the limits until one of those two calls comes. And a claim sweeps before it moves, so the total deployed can fall across a claim by other reservations' amounts

- Carry the same withdrawal into the article on setting money aside, which described the claim that moves a reservation into flight without saying nothing in Nexus makes it

- Split how far down the account is into its own article, the four drawdown figures having made the stage article outgrow its cap on the round it was written. Nexus keeps two figures for what the account is worth: the whole value, which includes what open positions are up or down, and a second counting only what has been closed. Each keeps a high mark that only rises. The moment's drawdown on the whole value falls back as the value recovers, where the worst on closed money is a record that stays. And the worst on the whole value is kept the same way and handed to no check, so a bad hour of open positions leaves a record nothing acts on

- Correct the risk stage's four drawdown figures, which are not one measure counted two ways twice: the first pair is the current drawdown on total equity and its share of that high water, and the second is the lifetime worst on realized equity and its share of its own, so unrealized profit is in the first pair and not the second

- Withdraw the claim that money is spent as a command goes out. At the pinned commit nothing under `nexus/` calls the step that would move a reservation into what sent orders hold — the function is exported and has no caller but the tests, the duty belonging to the host that wires Nexus to Praxis. What the source does show is the capital stage granting the reservation, and only an entry's answer carrying one, the stage that grants it being the stage an exit and a change skip

- Extend the renumbering check to a tally of part of a group, the form that slipped past it and was wrong four times over. A count of the whole set, which the code does name, stays allowed

- Stop counting which stages read the order, that count having been wrong twice now. Four of the six do: intake reads an entry's side and amount and a change's amount, capital reads the value and the fees, the platform ceilings read the value, and the price stage refuses a decision carrying no reference price where a deviation cap is set. What the stages mostly read is the state of the account

- Correct two more. A stopping host keeps only the exits and abandonments its strategies ask for, and an exit naming a trade the record has no position for is dropped before any send. And a change is asked only for an amount above nought, intake reading nothing about the amount it replaces

- Split what each check weighs out of the walk's article, which it had grown past the cap with three rounds of findings against it. The risk stage walks seven figures and stops at the first over its limit, an unset limit letting that figure run; the platform stage weighs the order's value and four account figures, and refuses where one it needs is absent, which is the opposite handling; the price stage reads the book's age, spread and a handed-in deviation; the health stage reads none of the decision at all; and the capital stage is the one that sets money aside

- Correct one more. A fill does not move one amount: it takes the filled amount and the fee estimated on it out of what resting orders hold, and puts that amount and the fee actually charged into what the position holds. The difference between the two fees is what goes to the reserve

- Correct one more. No action skips intake, which is not the same as every walk reaching it: the order is given rather than fixed, so a walk putting another stage first can refuse there and never get to intake

- Correct two more. The price stage reads no book price against the reference price: it refuses on the book's age, its spread, and a deviation handed in against a cap set per strategy. And a nought cost basis is read before an exit fill is weighed against the position, so an oversize fill on one raises rather than reducing it

- Correct two more. A stopping host does not close what is open: it sends the exits its strategies ask it for, and one asking for nothing leaves nothing to send. And the fee reserve is an entry's affair — an exit's fill never reaches it, that exit's fees coming off the profit it reports instead

- Correct four more. The risk stage weighs losses run up over rolling windows as well as drawdowns, and either alone refuses a decision. The two stages that read the order read what it comes to in money, not its size in coin. A refusal and a cancellation take money from whichever of the two holdings the order sits in, where an acknowledgement and a fill each want one and are refused for the other. And an entry fill whose position never moved is still handed to the strategy, the loop logging the raise and carrying on with that same outcome

- Correct three more. The cross-reference to the roads around the walk named two of them when a third had been added. An acknowledgement is refused unless the order is one sent and a fill unless it is one resting, so neither moves money from the other holding. And a reserve left within a rounding margin of nothing is set to nothing, so a small surplus need not lift it

- Split the fee reserve out of the entry's article, which it had grown past the cap. The reserve is money held back for what trading costs: one of five sums taken off the pool, moved by the gap between what a command's fees were estimated at and what the fill billed, drawn down by fills that cost more and topped up by ones that cost less, and refusing a fill's move where the shortfall is bigger than the reserve itself

- Correct seven more. A third kind of order reaches Praxis without the walk: a shutdown whose wait runs out abandons each command still pending, the same way. A refusal's message is required to be there and not blank, with nothing routing it to a person. An answer that is not a decision raises before the answer is read for anything else, rather than sparing a pass that would otherwise be counted. An acknowledgement runs for every kind and moves money for an entry alone. A refusal, cancellation or expiry of an entry reaches no position. And the dust path needs the fill to have finished the order, a part-fill leaving the remainder on the position
